Get SR\n    sr = metrics.get('overall_sr') or metrics.get('success_rate') or metrics.get('overall_SR') or metrics.get('avg_sr')\n    if sr is None:\n        continue\n    \n    eid_lower = eid.lower()\n    is_openvla = 'openvla' in eid_lower or 'openvla' in model_name.lower()\n    is_pi05 = 'pi05' in eid_lower or 'pi0.5' in model_name.lower() or 'pi05' in model_name.lower()\n    is_eval = 'eval' in eid_lower\n    \n    if not (is_openvla or is_pi05) or not is_eval:\n        continue\n    \n    # Extract method\n    method = ''\n    if 'l2norm' in eid_lower: method = 'l2norm'\n    elif 'attn' in eid_lower or 'attention' in eid_lower: method = 'attention'\n    elif 'random' in eid_lower: method = 'random'\n    elif 'wanda' in eid_lower: method = 'wanda'\n    \n    # Metrics may have prune_method\n    if not method:\n        method = metrics.get('prune_method', '') or spec.get('prune_method', '')\n    \n    # Extract k\n    k = None\n    # From metrics first\n    kr = metrics.get('keep_ratio') or spec.get('keep_ratio')\n    if kr:\n        k = float(kr)\n    else:\n        # From exp_id\n        k_match = re.search(r'k(\\d+\\.?\\d*)', eid_lower)\n        if k_match:\n            kstr = k_match.group(1)\n            if '.' in kstr:\n                k = float(kstr)\n            else:\n                # k05 -> 0.5, k075 -> 0.75, k085 -> 0.85, k09 -> 0.9, k095 -> 0.95\n                if len(kstr) == 1: k = float('0.' + kstr)\n                elif len(kstr) == 2: k = float('0.' + kstr)\n                elif len(kstr) == 3: k = float('0.' + kstr)\n                else: k = float('0.' + kstr)\n    \n    if k is None:\n        continue\n    \n    # Determine reliability\n    reliable = 'yes'\n    # pi0.5 on cqa1 = contaminated (unless 'strict' in eid)\n    if model == 'pi0.5' and 'cqa1' in server:\n        if 'strict' not in eid_lower:\n            reliable = 'no'\n    # Also check notes for contamination\n    if 'contaminated' in notes.lower() or 'lenient' in notes.lower():\n        reliable = 'no'\n    \n    # Determine if this is a superseded experiment (has v2, v3 etc)\n    #
